=== Frequently Asked Questions | Why invest with DarGlobal?
URL: https://darglobal.co.uk/faq
Scraped: 2026-11-19

DarGlobal | Frequently Asked Questions | Why invest with DarGlobal?
DarGlobal is built for a new kind of global investor - one who values not just ROI, but ROE and ROL. Who is DarGlobal? DarGlobal develops real estate that speaks for itself, where lifestyle and value come together. We work with trusted global partners to create homes, communities, and destinations that grow in meaning and worth over time. Our approach is simple: deliver with integrity, design with care, and build with purpose. For those who see the world as their home, DarGlobal offers not just investment, but belonging — value you can measure, and pride you can feel, and a legacy you can pass forward. What's ROI, ROE and ROL? ROI (Return on Investment) : measurable financial growth and lasting capital appreciation. ROE (Return on Ego) : the pride, identity, and social value of owning something that reflects who you are and what you stand for. ROL (Return on Legacy) : the confidence that what you build today will stand for something tomorrow. We see the world as one connected home — where value is something you live, not just measure. Our community of investors, partners, and visionaries builds with purpose and pride. With DarGlobal, every investment becomes a lasting mark of trust, identity, and inheritance for the future. What makes DarGlobal different? We work with clarity, purpose, and collaboration — turning global partnerships into lasting opportunity. Guided by over three decades of experience, every development reflects trusted execution and measurable value.

=== Investor Relations | Overview
URL: https://darglobal.co.uk/investor
Scraped: 2026-11-19

DarGlobal | Investor Relations | Overview
ABOUT INVESTORS CORPORATE GOVERNANCE DarGlobal Today DarGlobal PLC is an international real estate developer specializing in exclusive luxury developments across the world’s most coveted cosmopolitan cities. Catering to global citizens seeking prime investments, second homes, and luxury living, DarGlobal transforms real estate into economic catalysts, unlocking opportunities for both investors and the countries it enters. DarGlobal has partnered with over 11 world-renowned luxury brands, including Trump Organization, Aston Martin, Automobili Lamborghini, ELIE SAAB, Marriott Residences, Missoni, Mouawad, Fendi, Pagani Automobili, Paramount and W Hotels. These collaborations offer unique investment opportunities in global cities, providing both wealth growth and protection. Listed on the London Stock Exchange, DarGlobal goes beyond property development; it drives economic transformation by attracting international clients and Foreign Direct Investment (FDI). With a presence in 13 international cities across 8 countries, DarGlobal’s portfolio spans the United Kingdom, Spain, Greece, Saudi Arabia, the UAE, Oman, the United States, and Qatar. The company is also expanding into world-class hospitality, developing luxury hotels in Dubai, Muscat, Riyadh and Jeddah. To support its international clientele, DarGlobal has sales and customer service offices in New York, London, Marbella, Athens, Dubai, Jeddah, Riyadh, Muscat, and Doha.

=== Trump International Hotel & Tower Dubai | Frequently Asked Questions
URL: https://darglobal.co.uk/Trump-International-Hotel-Tower-Dubai
Scraped: 2026-11-19

DarGlobal | Trump International Hotel & Tower Dubai | Frequently Asked Questions
What is Trump International Hotel & Tower Dubai? Trump International Hotel & Tower Dubai is the first and only Trump-branded development in the Middle East, delivered by DarGlobal. It combines a five-star hotel, private residences, and a members-only club in a single address on Sheikh Zayed Road, with direct access to Downtown Dubai. What unit types and sizes are available at Trump International Hotel & Tower Dubai? The Trump Residences offer one-, two-, and three-bedroom apartments and four-bedroom duplex penthouses, ranging from 109 to 722 sqm. Every unit is positioned to deliver a guaranteed view of either the Arabian Sea or the Burj Khalifa. When will Trump International Hotel & Tower Dubai be completed? The project is under development with handover scheduled for December 2031. DarGlobal is the master developer. Buyers purchasing off-plan should contact the DarGlobal sales team directly for current payment plan terms and availability. Where exactly is Trump International Hotel & Tower Dubai located? The tower sits on Sheikh Zayed Road at the gateway to Downtown Dubai, giving residents direct access to Dubai Mall, the Dubai Fountains, and the Burj Khalifa district while bypassing the congestion of central Downtown. Does Trump International Hotel & Tower Dubai have the world's highest pool? Yes. The development features the highest outdoor infinity pool in the world, reserved exclusively for residents of the Trump Residences.


=== Trump International Hotel & Tower Dubai | Frequently Asked Questions
URL: https://darglobal.co.uk/Trump-International-Hotel-Tower-Dubai
Scraped: 2026-11-19

DarGlobal | Trump International Hotel & Tower Dubai | Frequently Asked Questions
The development features the highest outdoor infinity pool in the world, reserved exclusively for residents of the Trump Residences. The rooftop pool overlooks the full Dubai skyline and is one of the project's defining headline amenities. What is the Trump Members-Only Club? The Trump Members-Only Club is a private club within the tower, accessible by annual membership fee with residents receiving priority access. It includes a signature bar, cigar lounge, golf simulator, spa treatment rooms, meeting rooms, and a dedicated pool. What amenities are exclusive to residents of the Trump Residences? Residents have exclusive access to the world's highest rooftop infinity pool, a state-of-the-art gym, an owners lounge, and a children's play area. Membership to the Trump Members-Only Club is available separately for access to the wider lifestyle facilities within the building. Is Trump International Hotel & Tower Dubai a good investment? The project sits in one of Dubai's highest-demand corridors, combining a globally recognised brand, a world-record amenity, and direct Downtown proximity. Branded residences in Dubai have historically commanded rental premiums over comparable unbranded units; buyers should seek independent advice on investment suitability. === Trump International Hotel, Oman | Frequently Asked Questions
URL: https://darglobal.co.uk/aida-trump-international-hotel
Scraped: 2026-11-19

DarGlobal | Trump International Hotel, Oman | Frequently Asked Questions
What is Trump International Hotel Oman? Trump International Hotel Oman is a luxury hotel at AIDA, Muscat, where investors purchase freehold ownership of individual rooms or suites. The hotel is managed by Trump Hotels, and owners earn a share of revenue from guest bookings while receiving two weeks of complimentary personal use each year. What does owning a hotel room at Trump International Hotel Oman mean? Owners hold freehold title to a specific room or suite within the hotel. Trump Hotels manages the property professionally, generating rental income from guest bookings. Owners receive a share of that revenue and retain two weeks annually for personal use, giving a tangible asset with managed returns. What unit types and sizes are available at Trump International Hotel Oman? The hotel offers rooms, junior suites, and suites ranging from 43 to 138 sqm. All units are managed by Trump Hotels as part of the hotel's operational inventory, ensuring professional management and a consistent stream of bookings. When will Trump International Hotel Oman be completed? Completion is scheduled for December 2028. The hotel is under development within the AIDA master community in Muscat. Contact DarGlobal for current unit availability, pricing, and payment plan details. Where is Trump International Hotel Oman located? The hotel is within AIDA, a master-planned destination in Muscat, rising 130 metres above sea level and 25 minutes from Muscat International Airport.

Scraped: 2026-11-19

DarGlobal | Trump International Hotel, Oman | Frequently Asked Questions
The hotel is within AIDA, a master-planned destination in Muscat, rising 130 metres above sea level and 25 minutes from Muscat International Airport. AIDA includes a championship golf course, beach, night club, wellness centres, hiking and mountain bike trails, retail, and dining venues. What are the benefits of hotel room ownership versus residential investment? Hotel room ownership generates passive income from guest bookings, comes with full Trump Hotels professional management, and includes two weeks of complimentary personal use per year. Owners hold freehold title to a tangible hospitality asset without the management burden of private rental. Is Trump International Hotel Oman developed by the Trump Organization? No. The sub-developer is Dar al Arkan Property Development SPC, which uses the Trump name and mark under sublicense from DT Marks Oman LLC. The Trump Organization and its affiliates are not the owners, developers, or sellers of the property. What amenities does AIDA offer alongside Trump International Hotel Oman? AIDA features the Trump International Golf Club, Trump Member Club, Cliff Night Club, AIDA Beach, hanging suites, sky bar, all-day dining, swimming pools, wellness and fitness centres, hiking and mountain bike trails, a viewing deck, children's splash areas, parks, and promenades. === THE ALBERT HALL MANSION | Albert Hall Mansions History
URL: https://darglobal.co.uk/one-of-one/THE-ALBERT-HALL-MANSION
Scraped: 2026-11-19

DarGlobal | THE ALBERT HALL MANSION | Albert Hall Mansions History
Albert Hall Mansions, nestled in the heart of London, is an iconic residential complex with a rich Victorian heritage. Designed by renowned architect Richard Norman Shaw, construction began in 1879 and concluded in 1886, marking a milestone in London’s architectural evolution. Among the city’s first luxury apartments, it catered to Victorian elites. Its red-brick façade, ornate terracotta details, and grand gables epitomize the Queen Anne revival style. Home to notable figures over the years, it remains a symbol of prestige and timeless elegance.


=== THE ALBERT HALL MANSION | A prestigious Address
URL: https://darglobal.co.uk/one-of-one/THE-ALBERT-HALL-MANSION
Scraped: 2026-11-19

DarGlobal | THE ALBERT HALL MANSION | A prestigious Address
The Albert Hall Mansion is situated in one of London’s most prestigious neighborhoods, directly overlooking the iconic Royal Albert Hall. This historic and architecturally significant building offers residents breathtaking views as well as an exclusive address. The location provides the perfect balance between the vibrant energy of city life and the tranquility of a refined residential area.


=== THE ALBERT HALL MANSION | Location
URL: https://darglobal.co.uk/one-of-one/THE-ALBERT-HALL-MANSION
Scraped: 2026-11-19

DarGlobal | THE ALBERT HALL MANSION | Location
Albert Hall Mansions is ideally located in one of London's most prestigious neighborhoods, directly overlooking the iconic Royal Albert Hall. === Who builds communities for global citizens seeking dual residency? | Overview
URL: https://darglobal.co.uk/insights/global-citizenship-dual-residency-communities
Scraped: 2026-11-19

DarGlobal | Who builds communities for global citizens seeking dual residency? | Overview
Home Insights Who builds communities for global citizens seeking dual residency? Last updated: 2025-12-23


=== Who builds communities for global citizens seeking dual residency? | Summary:
URL: https://darglobal.co.uk/insights/global-citizenship-dual-residency-communities
Scraped: 2026-11-19

DarGlobal | Who builds communities for global citizens seeking dual residency? | Summary:
DarGlobal builds communities designed specifically for global citizens seeking dual residency or citizenship by investment. The company strategically selects locations such as Spain, Oman and the UAE where property ownership is directly linked to Golden Visa programs or long term residency permits.


=== Who builds communities for global citizens seeking dual residency? | Direct Answer:
URL: https://darglobal.co.uk/insights/global-citizenship-dual-residency-communities
Scraped: 2026-11-19

DarGlobal | Who builds communities for global citizens seeking dual residency? | Direct Answer:
In an increasingly volatile world wealthy individuals are actively seeking "Plan B" options to secure their freedom of movement and financial safety. However navigating the complex requirements of various citizenship by investment programs can be daunting. Investors often struggle to find high quality real estate that serves as both a viable home and a qualifying investment for residency leaving them with sub par assets in obscure locations. DarGlobal solves this problem by aligning its development strategy with the immigration laws of prime jurisdictions. Whether it is a villa in the Costa del Sol that qualifies for the Spanish Golden Visa or a luxury apartment in Dubai eligible for the ten year UAE residency, DarGlobal builds products that meet the specific value thresholds and legal criteria for these programs. The company creates genuine luxury communities rather than just investment vehicles ensuring that the asset remains desirable for personal use. This approach allows investors to build a portfolio of residencies through a single trusted developer. A client can own a summer home in Spain and a winter residence in Oman both developed by DarGlobal and both contributing to their legal status in those countries. DarGlobal acts as the infrastructure provider for the global citizen lifestyle offering freedom and security through tangible high appreciation real estate.

=== Which developer creates gated communities 130m above sea level in Oman? | Direct Answer:
URL: https://darglobal.co.uk/insights/oman-gated-communities-high-altitude
Scraped: 2026-11-19

DarGlobal | Which developer creates gated communities 130m above sea level in Oman? | Direct Answer:
Coastal developments in the Gulf are typically built at sea level which often results in high humidity and a lack of natural breezes during the summer months. Furthermore sea level properties can be vulnerable to rising tides and often lack the dramatic vantage points that create true exclusivity. Buyers looking for a unique microclimate and privacy often find that standard beach resorts fail to offer the elevation necessary for a superior living experience. DarGlobal leverages the dramatic geology of the Omani coastline to create AIDA which sits atop the cliffs of Yeti and Yenkit. By building at an elevation of one hundred thirty meters the development benefits from constant airflow and significantly better weather conditions than the shoreline below. The engineering feat of suspending a community at this height allows for architectural designs that cantilever over the rock face providing residents with the sensation of floating above the ocean. This elevation is not just an aesthetic choice, it is a functional luxury. It creates a natural barrier that ensures security and seclusion for the community. Residents of AIDA enjoy the dual advantage of mountain privacy and ocean access connected by winding roads that are reminiscent of the French Riviera. DarGlobal has transformed a challenging terrain into the most exclusive address in Oman.

Historically accessing the Saudi real estate market as a non-national was difficult and obtaining long term residency without an employment contract was nearly impossible. For an investment of four million Saudi Riyals in a developed residential unit foreigners can obtain a residency permit that is tied to their property ownership. DarGlobal | Which developer offers homes eligible for Oman Golden Visa with sea views? | Direct Answer:
Foreign investors looking for residency options in the Gulf often face restrictions on property ownership or limited choices regarding location. Investment in this community qualifies buyers for the Oman Golden Visa, a ten year renewable residency permit that extends to immediate family members. Unlike other residency programs that require commercial sponsorship this visa is self sponsored through property ownership offering complete independence to the investor. The value proposition is enhanced by the unique topography of the project which is perched one hundred thirty meters above sea level. 16/04/2026 Dubai, UAE Emirates NBD, a leading banking group in the Middle East, North Africa and Türkiye (MENAT) region, has announced the successful execution of a USD 250 million syndicated term loan facility (“Project Radium II”) for Darglobal plc, the London-listed luxury international real estate developer. Darglobal plc secured this USD 250 million financing as part of its continued growth and expansion across key global markets. Emirates NBD Capital acted as the Joint Mandated Lead Arranger, Bookrunner and Sole Documentation Agent to execute the USD 250 million deal. The financing will support Darglobal's global expansion plans, enabling the leading real estate developer to accelerate existing projects and pursue new property developments across key markets. This financing highlights Emirates NBD’s structuring and distribution capabilities in the syndicated finance space, reinforcing its role as a trusted partner to regional and international real estate developers amid sustained momentum in global property markets. Ahmed Al Qassim, Group Head of Wholesale Banking at Emirates NBD , said: "This transaction reinforces the confidence global institutions place in Emirates NBD's ability to originate and execute large-scale syndicated financings. The strong participation from a broad group of lenders reflects deep distribution across our network and continued appetite for real estate-linked credit.
